## 2026-06-30 - `cds watch` blank-screen investigation

| Classification | Symptom | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Data/environment issue | After starting `cds watch` on `dev`, the email worker repeatedly fails with `SQLITE_ERROR: no such table: idts_cap_NotificationDeliveries`. | Confirmed: `db/schema.cds` contains the IDTS-36 entity, but the local `db.sqlite` table inventory contains the older notification tables and not `idts_cap_NotificationDeliveries`; the DB file predates the merge. | Diagnosed, not yet changed because a normal SQLite `cds deploy` dry run shows DROP/CREATE for the full local schema and can replace local-only data. | Stop `cds watch`, back up `db.sqlite`, then either recreate the development DB from seeds (recommended if local data is disposable) or implement/test schema evolution if local data must be preserved. |
| Product integration defect on `dev` | Opening `/bug-management-ui/webapp/index.html` produces a blank page while the OData metadata and `/odata/v4/auth/me` requests return `401 Unauthorized`. | Confirmed integration mismatch: `dev` contains the IDTS-34 protected backend but does not contain PR #30's `login.html`, `login-page.js`, or synchronous `auth-guard.js`. The current `Component.js` starts Fiori immediately, so the first `/odata/v4/bug/$metadata` request has no bearer token and is rejected; Fiori Elements cannot build the List Report without metadata. | Diagnosed, not yet fixed under structured-debug approval rule. | Finish/review and merge PR #30 (or temporarily test its branch), then verify unauthenticated index redirects to login, login stores a token, and the first metadata request includes the bearer token. |
| Tooling issue | The first local UI inspection command attempted to read `auth-guard.js` and `login-page.js` from `dev` and passed `*.js`/`*.html` paths directly to `rg`; those files are not present on `dev` and Windows rejected the wildcard paths. | Inspection command assumed the unmerged PR #30 file layout and used Unix-like path globbing on PowerShell. | Fixed for the investigation by listing tracked files first and using `rg --glob` or explicit paths. No product file or credential was affected. | Inspect the actual `dev` Component/manifest/login artifacts and compare them with PR #30 separately. |
| Test-harness/tooling issue | The first read-only SQLite table-list command failed with a JavaScript syntax error before opening the database. | Nested PowerShell/JavaScript quoting corrupted the SQL string passed to `node -e`. | Fixed by switching to a PowerShell here-string piped to Node. No database write occurred. | Rerun the table-list query read-only and continue schema comparison. |
| Test-harness/tooling issue | The combined SQLite/HTTP probe was rejected by the PowerShell parser with `An empty pipe element is not allowed`. | The command attempted to pipe directly from a completed `foreach` block instead of assigning its output first. | Fixed in the probe by collecting results in `$results` before formatting. The command stopped before any database or HTTP operation. | Rerun the corrected read-only probe. |
| Environment/dependency issue | The first post-merge-result regression run in the PR #30 worktree stopped with `Cannot find module 'nodemailer'`. | PR #30 was merged locally with current `origin/dev`, which added Nodemailer to `package.json`, but that worktree's existing `node_modules` still reflected the older dependency set. | Fixed by running `npm ci` in the PR worktree; this is dependency synchronization, not a product code change. | Rerun compile, regression, auth, email, UI5 build and quality gates from the beginning after install. |
| Dependency/security issue | Fresh `npm ci` reported 20 known vulnerabilities in the full dependency tree: 18 moderate and 2 high. | Production-only audit narrowed runtime exposure to 6 moderate findings in the existing `@cap-js/attachments` -> `@google-cloud/storage`/`retry-request`/`teeny-request`/`uuid` chain; the 2 high findings are dev-only. The suggested automatic fix would downgrade/change the direct attachment package and is not safe to apply blindly. | Open as a separate dependency-maintenance issue; no forced fix applied. It does not originate from PR #30 login code. | Track and review the attachment dependency upgrade separately with regression tests for S3 upload/download. Current login merge may proceed if all functional gates pass. |
| Local data issue | SQLite schema evolution successfully added `idts_cap_NotificationDeliveries` and retained 4 Bugs/4 Users, but the seed phase reset the one local `passwordHash` to empty. | `cds deploy` reapplied the tracked Users seed dataset, which intentionally contains no private password hash, after evolving the schema. | Fixed from the pre-deploy backup by restoring only the matching user's hash/timestamp without displaying the value. | Verify one user again has a non-empty hash and perform a real local login/browser smoke. Keep the timestamped DB backup until verification finishes. |
| Dependency/configuration warning | Fresh merged-dev startup reports `cds.requires.<srv>.outbox` is deprecated and recommends `cds.requires.<srv>.outboxed`. | A dependency or current effective CAP configuration still uses the legacy outbox property name; exact owner is not yet isolated. | Open, non-blocking for PR #30 and the SQLite/login fix. | Locate the effective config source and handle as a focused CAP compatibility change with email/outbox regression tests; do not mix it into the login merge. |
| Test-harness issue | The first merged-dev browser smoke completed redirect, invalid login, valid login, metadata 200, Fiori render and logout, but exited 1 because it treated all UI5 console `error` messages as blocking; the first classifier rerun then retained the intentional invalid-login 401 as a later blocking error. | The smoke did not separate expected negative-test output from the valid-login/render phase and initially did not account for local-development misses such as `Component-preload.js` 404 or unavailable LREP flex endpoints; no page exception occurred. | Fixed in the one-off smoke classifier by clearing captured console errors after the intentional invalid-login assertion, then allowlisting only known non-blocking local-preview messages. | Rerun strict validation for valid login, metadata 200, app render and logout with zero unclassified errors. |

Vietnamese: Đã xác nhận hai root cause tách biệt: SQLite local chưa được cập nhật schema IDTS-36; đồng thời `dev` đã bảo vệ backend bằng IDTS-34 nhưng chưa có login UI/auth guard của PR #30, khiến request metadata không có token và bị 401 nên Fiori trắng. Chưa sửa code hoặc DB; cần backup DB trước nếu rebuild và cần duyệt/merge PR #30 để hoàn tất auth flow.

Final resolution: PR #30 was updated with current `dev`, verified, pushed, and merged as `207c773`. The local SQLite database was backed up, evolved with `schema_evolution=auto`, and verified to contain `idts_cap_NotificationDeliveries` while retaining 4 Bugs and 4 Users. One password hash overwritten by seed deployment was restored from backup without exposing its value. Fresh merged-dev browser smoke passed unauthenticated redirect, safe invalid login, valid login, OData metadata 200, Fiori render, and logout; server logs contained zero missing-table/outbox-worker failures.

Vietnamese: Kết quả cuối: PR #30 đã được cập nhật theo `dev`, verify, push và merge tại commit `207c773`. SQLite local đã được backup, evolve schema bằng `schema_evolution=auto`, có bảng `idts_cap_NotificationDeliveries` và vẫn giữ 4 Bugs/4 Users. Password hash bị seed ghi đè đã được phục hồi từ backup mà không lộ giá trị. Browser smoke trên merged `dev` pass redirect khi chưa login, login sai an toàn, login đúng, metadata OData 200, Fiori render và logout; log server không còn lỗi thiếu bảng/email worker.

## 2026-06-30 - IDTS-36 real SMTP verification

English:

| Classification | Symptom | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Tooling issue | The first redacted SMTP readiness command did not start because the orchestration script contained PowerShell here-string syntax inside JavaScript. | Command-construction error in the Codex tool call; no application code or private configuration was executed or printed. | Fixed in session by switching to a plain command string. | Rerun the redacted readiness check, then continue with the isolated real-provider SMTP smoke test. |
| Environment/configuration blocker | The first redacted readiness check found all SMTP values present, but runtime flags remained `enabled=false` and `testMode=false`; therefore the configuration was intentionally not ready to send. | The private configuration retained the safe disabled defaults for the two control flags. | Fixed by setting `enabled=true` and `testMode=true`; no credential value was printed. | Fresh redacted rerun: `enabled=true`, `ready=true`, `testMode=true`, no missing fields. Continue with the isolated real Brevo smoke test. |
| External SMTP integration verification | Real-provider smoke with private Brevo SMTP config processed one test delivery from `PENDING` to `SENT`; `attemptCount=1`; `sentAt` and provider message-id were recorded; no safe error remained. | Not an error; this is the final private-provider acceptance proof for the IDTS-36 outbox path. | Passed. No credential, real recipient, private SMTP value, or provider message-id was copied into docs or Jira text. | Fresh smoke output ended with `IDTS-36 Brevo real-provider smoke: PASS`. User should still check inbox/spam because provider acceptance does not guarantee final mailbox placement. |
| Tooling issue | The first skill-read command used the external backup skill root for `karpathy-guidelines` and `verify`, where those two repo-local skills do not exist. | Skill-root selection error in the Codex tool call; the repo-local skills are under `E:\IDTS-SAP01\.agents\skills`. | Fixed in session by reading the correct repo-local skill paths before continuing. | Correct `karpathy-guidelines` and `verify` skill files were read from `E:\IDTS-SAP01\.agents\skills`; no product code was affected. |

## 2026-07-01 - IDTS-43 Fiori UX cleanup support

English:

| Classification | Symptom | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Tooling issue | Initial skill-read command used `C:\Users\LapHub\.agents\skills\...` for repo-local skills and failed with path-not-found. | Skill root selection error; the active repo-local skill root is `E:\IDTS-SAP01\.agents\skills`. | Fixed immediately by rereading `karpathy-guidelines`, `sap-fiori`, `sap-ui5`, `sap-fiori-guidelines`, and `verify` from the repo-local root. | Correct skill files were read before continuing; no product code was affected. |
| Tooling/MCP limitation | Fiori/UI5 MCP namespaces did not appear after focused `tool_search` calls; only CAP MCP and general Node/GitHub/Atlassian tools were exposed in this session. | Deferred MCP availability limitation in the current Codex tool context, not a product issue. | Work continued using the repo-local Fiori/UI5 skills, SAP Fiori Guidelines references, CAP MCP for CAP/Fiori metadata context, and local compile/build/static QA. | Record as limitation; use UI5 build/static tests/browser smoke for verification. |
| Tooling issue | Earlier IDTS-43 inspection attempted to read `app/bug-management-ui/webapp/model/LoginSession.js`, which does not exist. | Wrong assumption about the login helper path; current login helper is `app/bug-management-ui/webapp/ext/login/LoginController.js`. | Fixed by using the actual helper path through `BugListActions.js` dependency `../login/LoginController`. | Static IDTS-43 test validates the action module dependency and role behavior. |
| Verification/tooling issue | First UI5 build command ran from repo root with `--config ui5.yaml` and failed because the root has no `ui5.yaml` and root `package.json` has no UI5 app `name`. | Wrong working directory/config path for a workspace app. The actual UI5 project config is `app/bug-management-ui/ui5.yaml`. | Fixed by rerunning UI5 build from `app/bug-management-ui`. | Use `npx ui5 build --config ui5.yaml` with workdir `app/bug-management-ui`. |
| Verification issue | `git diff --check` found trailing whitespace in `IDTS-PROJECT-SCOPE-SAP01.md`. | Markdown line-break spacing introduced while updating the canonical no-auto-assign clarification. | Fixed by removing the trailing spaces and rerunning `git diff --check`. | Rerun diff check before commit. |
| Test-harness/tooling issue | First secret scan command failed with an unclosed regex character class in PowerShell. | The quoted regex mixed single/double quote escaping incorrectly. | Fixed by replacing it with multiple simpler `rg` patterns. | Rerun secret scan with safe simple patterns and manually review any documentation placeholder hits. |
| Tooling issue | Local CAP startup logged `cds-plugin-ui5` error: failed to inject application pages into the welcome page. | UI5 preview/welcome-page integration issue; direct app URL remains available. | Not fixed in IDTS-43 because it is not part of the product flow being changed. | Browser smoke uses the direct app URL `/bug-management-ui/webapp/login.html`; keep this as a non-blocking tooling note unless welcome-page links become part of QA scope. |
| Test-harness issue | First browser smoke failed because the classifier treated local UI5 preview console errors as blocking: LREP flex 404, S/CUBE unsupported, and a UI5 pseudo-module deprecation. | The smoke harness did not yet allowlist known non-product local-preview noise. The application startup message itself says it continues without LREP storage data. | Fixed by expanding the smoke allowlist and rerunning the role/create checks. | Rerun must still fail on page exceptions and HTTP 5xx, while ignoring only the known local-preview messages. |

Chính message startup cho biết app vẫn tiếp tục khi không có LREP storage data. | Đã sửa bằng cách mở allowlist và rerun role/create checks. | Lần rerun vẫn phải fail nếu có page exception hoặc HTTP 5xx; chỉ ignore đúng các local-preview message đã biết. |

Additional IDTS-43 issue observed during continuation:

English:

| Classification | Symptom | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Product runtime defect | Browser smoke showed Developer does not see Create and Tester/PM can see the custom Create Bug action, but PM clicking it raised `this.getView is not a function`. | The custom List Report action handler assumed a controller-style `this.getView()` context. Fiori Elements invoked the handler with an ExtensionAPI-like context that exposes `editFlow` and `_view`, but no `getView()` method. | Under fix in the IDTS-43 branch before PR/merge. | Update `BugListActions.js` and the focused static test to match the actual Fiori action runtime context, then rerun role-based browser smoke. |
| Test-harness issue | The first rerun of `npm run qa:idts43:programmatic` after the runtime-context fix passed the main create-path checks but failed the new missing-model negative case. | The test used `assert.rejects` for a synchronous guard error thrown before a Promise is returned. This is a test assertion mismatch, not a product runtime failure. | Fixed in the focused test by using `assert.throws` for the synchronous guard. | Rerun `npm run qa:idts43:programmatic` and require `11 PASS / 0 FAIL`. |
| Test-harness issue | The next browser smoke rerun stopped at the Developer scenario because Chrome emitted generic 404/MIME console errors for missing `Component-preload.js`. | The classifier only allowlisted some local-preview message shapes. The actual browser message did not include enough URL detail, while the response URL identifies this as the known UI5 preload fallback. | Fixed in the one-off smoke by validating HTTP responses separately and allowing only known local-preview 404s such as `Component-preload.js`, while keeping page errors and HTTP 5xx blocking. | Rerun role smoke; it must reach PM click and show no `this.getView` page error. |
| Test-harness issue | The response-based browser smoke then stopped on `404 .../i18n/i18n_en_US.properties` before reaching the Create click. | SAPUI5 requests the most specific locale bundle first and falls back to the existing `i18n_en.properties`; this is expected local UI5 i18n fallback behavior. | Fixed in the one-off smoke by allowlisting only the locale-specific i18n fallback path while still failing unexpected app/API responses. | Rerun role smoke through Developer, Tester, and PM click. |
| Tooling issue | A follow-up inspection command tried to read `app/bug-management-ui/webapp/ext/login/login-page.js`, which does not exist. | The standalone login page script lives at `app/bug-management-ui/webapp/login-page.js`; `ext/login/LoginController.js` is a different session helper. | Fixed by reading the correct root-level login page script. | No product code affected; keep path distinction in future UI inspections. |
| Product runtime defect | The `this.getView is not a function` Create Bug click failure was reproduced by browser smoke and then fixed. | `BugListActions.js` now isolates runtime-context access in `getModelFromActionContext(...)` and still uses Fiori `editFlow.createDocument(...)` for creation. | Fixed in branch. | Fresh browser smoke: `PASS Developer: createVisible=false`, `PASS Tester: createVisible=true`, `PASS PM: createVisible=true, clickCreate=ok`, `IDTS-43 browser smoke: PASS`. |
| PM/process update | IDTS-43 Fiori cleanup was reviewed, committed, pushed, opened as PR #36, and merged into `dev`. | Not an error; this records the final handoff state after implementation. | Complete. | Merge commit `62197e6`; follow-up PM board update records IDTS-43 as Done. |

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Loi runtime san pham | Browser smoke cho thay Developer khong thay Create va Tester/PM thay custom action Create Bug, nhung khi PM bam nut thi phat sinh loi `this.getView is not a function`. | Handler custom action dang gia dinh context giong controller co `this.getView()`. Fiori Elements goi handler bang context giong ExtensionAPI, co `editFlow` va `_view` nhung khong co `getView()`. | Dang fix trong branch IDTS-43 truoc khi tao PR/merge. | Cap nhat `BugListActions.js` va focused static test de bat dung runtime context cua Fiori action, sau do chay lai browser smoke theo role. |
| Loi test-harness | Lan chay lai dau tien cua `npm run qa:idts43:programmatic` sau fix runtime-context da pass cac check create-path chinh nhung fail negative case khi thieu model. | Test dung `assert.rejects` cho loi guard synchronous duoc throw truoc khi co Promise. Day la sai cach assert trong test, khong phai loi runtime san pham. | Da fix trong focused test bang `assert.throws` cho synchronous guard. | Chay lai `npm run qa:idts43:programmatic` va yeu cau `11 PASS / 0 FAIL`. |
| Loi test-harness | Lan browser smoke tiep theo dung o scenario Developer vi Chrome emit console error generic 404/MIME cho `Component-preload.js`. | Classifier chi allowlist mot so dang message local-preview. Message browser thuc te khong co du URL, trong khi response URL cho thay day la UI5 preload fallback da biet. | Da fix trong one-off smoke bang cach validate HTTP response rieng va chi allowlist 404 local-preview da biet nhu `Component-preload.js`, van fail voi page error va HTTP 5xx. | Chay lai role smoke; phai toi duoc buoc PM click va khong con page error `this.getView`. |
| Loi test-harness | Browser smoke dua tren response sau do dung o `404 .../i18n/i18n_en_US.properties` truoc khi toi buoc Create click. | SAPUI5 thu bundle ngon ngu cu the nhat truoc roi fallback ve file dang co `i18n_en.properties`; day la hanh vi i18n fallback local hop le. | Da fix trong one-off smoke bang cach chi allowlist locale-specific i18n fallback path, van fail voi response app/API bat thuong. | Chay lai role smoke qua Developer, Tester va PM click. |
| Loi tooling | Lenh inspection tiep theo thu doc `app/bug-management-ui/webapp/ext/login/login-page.js`, nhung file nay khong ton tai. | Script standalone cua login page nam tai `app/bug-management-ui/webapp/login-page.js`; `ext/login/LoginController.js` la session helper khac. | Da fix bang cach doc dung script login page o root webapp. | Khong anh huong product code; can tach ro hai path nay khi inspect UI login. |
| Loi runtime san pham | Loi click Create Bug `this.getView is not a function` da duoc reproduce bang browser smoke va da fix. | `BugListActions.js` da tach logic lay runtime context vao `getModelFromActionContext(...)` va van dung Fiori `editFlow.createDocument(...)` de tao draft. | Da fix trong branch. | Browser smoke moi: `PASS Developer: createVisible=false`, `PASS Tester: createVisible=true`, `PASS PM: createVisible=true, clickCreate=ok`, `IDTS-43 browser smoke: PASS`. |

## 2026-07-01 - IDTS-42 QA Depth Gate implementation

English:

| Classification | Symptom | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Test-harness issue | First `npm run qa:depth:self-test` run failed in the bare `N/A` case even though valid body and missing-section checks passed. | The newly added self-test expected a specific error message, but the validator classified bare `N/A` as too-thin content before reaching the explicit `N/A must include a reason` branch. | Fixed by checking bare `N/A` before minimum content length. | Fresh rerun: `QA Depth Gate self-test: 5 PASS / 0 FAIL`. |
| Environment/dependency issue | First CAP compile/auth/IDTS-41 verification in the new IDTS-42 worktree failed with missing `@sap/cds` / `@cap-js/attachments`. | The isolated worktree was created from Git only and did not have `node_modules`; this is workspace dependency setup, not a product code failure. | Fixed for local verification by using the existing root workspace dependency install through a local `node_modules` junction in the worktree. | Rerun CAP compile and programmatic QA after dependency link is available. |
| Test-harness/tooling issue | First PR-body stdin verification used `npm run qa:depth:pr-body -- --stdin` in a PowerShell pipeline and the validator reported every required section missing. | `npm` did not pass the `--stdin` argument to the script in this invocation shape and consumed the piped input before `check-pr-depth.js` read it. | Fixed for verification by invoking `node scripts/qa/check-pr-depth.js --stdin` directly; the workflow uses `--event`, so it is not affected. | Fresh direct rerun: `QA Depth Gate PR body check: PASS (9 required sections)`. Keep npm script for normal no-argument/event usage. |
| Process/test-harness issue | PR template said `Known Gaps` may be `None`, but the validator treated short content as too thin. | The gate was stricter than its own template for this one section. | Fixed by allowing exactly `None` only in `Known Gaps`; all other required sections still need evidence text or explained `N/A`. | Covered by self-test where `Known Gaps` is `None` and the valid PR body still passes. |
| Test-harness issue | Direct PR-body verification failed when an evidence sentence mentioned “Known Gaps value None” inside `Edge/Boundary Evidence`. | The untested-claim detector used a broad `none` substring match and treated explanatory text as if the whole section claimed no testing. | Fixed by detecting `None`, `not tested`, or `no tests` only when they appear as explicit untested claims. | Self-test now includes the phrase “Known Gaps value None” in edge evidence and still passes. |
| Tooling issue | `gh pr merge 38 --merge --delete-branch` failed locally with `fatal: 'dev' is already used by worktree at 'E:/IDTS-SAP01'`. | GitHub CLI attempted a local Git checkout/update that conflicts with the root repository worktree already using branch `dev`. | Fixed by merging PR #38 through the GitHub API, which does not require local checkout. | API merge returned merge commit `1181289d5a9753b7ef857912437f1f4d1192e1ad`; remote feature branch was deleted through GitHub API. |
| Process/tooling issue | After enabling branch protection, `qa-depth-gate` was required but the workflow still had a path filter. Docs-only PRs could become blocked because the required check might not run. | The original workflow was designed to gate only runtime/QA-path changes; once the check became required at branch level, it must appear on every PR to `dev`. | Fixed by removing the workflow path filter so `qa-depth-gate` runs on all PRs into `dev`. | Verify with PR for this fix: required check must appear and pass before merge. |

Vietnamese:

| Phan loai | Trieu chung | Nguyen nhan | Trang thai fix | Verify / buoc tiep theo |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Loi test-harness | Lan chay dau tien `npm run qa:depth:self-test` fail o case `N/A` trong khi valid body va missing-section checks da pass. | Self-test moi mong dung mot message cu the, nhung validator xem bare `N/A` la content qua mong truoc khi toi nhanh `N/A must include a reason`. | Da fix bang cach check bare `N/A` truoc minimum content length. | Rerun moi: `QA Depth Gate self-test: 5 PASS / 0 FAIL`. |
| Loi moi truong/dependency | Lan verify CAP compile/auth/IDTS-41 dau tien trong worktree IDTS-42 fail vi thieu `@sap/cds` / `@cap-js/attachments`. | Worktree tach rieng moi tao tu Git nen chua co `node_modules`; day la setup dependency cua workspace, khong phai loi product code. | Da fix cho local verification bang cach dung dependency install co san o root workspace qua `node_modules` junction trong worktree. | Chay lai CAP compile va programmatic QA sau khi dependency link san sang. |
| Loi test-harness/tooling | Lan verify PR-body stdin dau tien dung `npm run qa:depth:pr-body -- --stdin` trong PowerShell pipeline va validator bao thieu tat ca section. | `npm` khong truyen `--stdin` vao script voi kieu invocation nay va piped input bi consume truoc khi `check-pr-depth.js` doc. | Da fix cho verification bang cach goi truc tiep `node scripts/qa/check-pr-depth.js --stdin`; workflow dung `--event` nen khong bi anh huong. | Rerun truc tiep moi: `QA Depth Gate PR body check: PASS (9 required sections)`. Giu npm script cho normal no-argument/event usage. |
| Loi process/test-harness | PR template noi `Known Gaps` co the ghi `None`, nhung validator lai xem noi dung ngan nay la qua mong. | Gate nghiem hon template cua chinh no o rieng section nay. | Da fix bang cach chi cho phep dung `None` trong `Known Gaps`; cac section bat buoc khac van phai co evidence hoac `N/A` co ly do. | Self-test da cover case valid PR body voi `Known Gaps` la `None`. |
| Loi test-harness | Direct PR-body verification fail khi cau evidence co nhac “Known Gaps value None” trong `Edge/Boundary Evidence`. | Bo phat hien claim chua test match substring `none` qua rong va hieu nham cau giai thich thanh claim ca section khong test. | Da fix bang cach chi bat `None`, `not tested`, hoac `no tests` khi chung la claim chua test ro rang. | Self-test da them phrase “Known Gaps value None” trong edge evidence va van pass. |
| Loi tooling | `gh pr merge 38 --merge --delete-branch` fail local voi `fatal: 'dev' is already used by worktree at 'E:/IDTS-SAP01'`. | GitHub CLI thu checkout/update local Git va bi conflict vi root repo dang dung branch `dev`. | Da fix bang cach merge PR #38 qua GitHub API, khong can checkout local. | API merge tra merge commit `1181289d5a9753b7ef857912437f1f4d1192e1ad`; remote feature branch da xoa qua GitHub API. |
| Loi process/tooling | Sau khi bat branch protection, `qa-depth-gate` la required check nhung workflow van co path filter. PR chi sua docs co the bi block vi required check khong chay. | Workflow ban dau chi gate runtime/QA-path changes; khi check tro thanh required o branch level thi no phai xuat hien tren moi PR vao `dev`. | Da fix bang cach bo path filter de `qa-depth-gate` chay tren tat ca PR vao `dev`. | Verify bang PR cho fix nay: required check phai xuat hien va pass truoc khi merge. |

## 2026-07-01 - IDTS-44 Render shared QA deployment prep

English:

| Classification | Symptom | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Environment/dependency issue | CAP MCP `search_model` failed in the fresh IDTS-44 worktree with `Can't find package module '@cap-js/attachments'`. | The isolated Git worktree does not contain ignored `node_modules`; this is local workspace setup, not a product failure. | Fixed for local verification by linking the existing root `node_modules` into the worktree. Product/source fix is not required. | Fresh CAP MCP `search_model` rerun returned service/entity names including `AuthService`, `BugService`, and `BugService.Bugs`. |
| Tooling issue | A trial `npx cds-serve --help` / `npx cds-deploy --help` command timed out and produced an EPIPE after `cds-deploy` interpreted `--help` as a deployment option. | `cds-serve` and `cds-deploy` are package binaries from `@sap/cds`, not the full `@sap/cds-dk` CLI help surface; `cds-deploy` expects deployment options such as credentials, not `--help`. | Fixed in session by inspecting the package binary files and using `cds-serve all` / `cds-deploy` only as runtime scripts. | Add Render scripts that use the production dependency binaries: `npm start`, `npm run render:db:deploy`, and `npm run render:auth:set-password`. |
| Tooling/config issue | First `npm install --package-lock-only` after adding Render scripts emitted `EBADENGINE` because `package.json` required Node `20.x` while the local machine uses Node `22.20.0`. | The initial engine pin was too narrow for the team's current local runtime, even though Render can run Node 20. | Fixed by changing the engine range to `>=20 <23`. | Fresh `npm install --package-lock-only` completed without the engine warning. |
| Dependency audit issue | `npm install --package-lock-only` reported 20 audit vulnerabilities: 18 moderate and 2 high. | Existing dependency tree audit state; this Render task did not add new packages, and automated `npm audit fix --force` may introduce broad or breaking dependency changes. | Open / out of scope for IDTS-44 unless DonHV approves a dedicated dependency security task. | Record for follow-up; do not run broad audit fix inside the Render deployment config PR. |
| Test-harness issue | GitHub `qa-depth-gate` failed PR #41 with `Missing required section: Summary` even though the PR body visibly had `## Summary`. | The PR body was created from a PowerShell UTF-8 file that included a BOM at the start; `check-pr-depth.js` did not strip the BOM before parsing Markdown headings. | Fixed in branch by stripping a leading UTF-8 BOM in the PR-body parser and adding a self-test case. | Rerun `npm run qa:depth:self-test`; expected result is now `6 PASS / 0 FAIL`, then push and rerun PR checks. |
| Process/deployment task | Render shared QA deploy prep started for Jira `IDTS-44`. | Team needs a shared URL for login, protected OData, PostgreSQL persistence, SMTP/outbox, and S3 attachments instead of relying on a local developer machine. | Implementation prep complete locally on branch `chore/idts-44-render-qa-deploy-donhv`; PR/merge remains next. | Added `render.yaml`, Render deploy docs, PM task file, task board/current-status/risk-decision updates. Local verification passed CAP compile, auth QA, email outbox QA, QA depth self-test, secret scan, AI DevKit lint, diff check, Render env checks, and `npm start` login-page smoke. |
| Deployment configuration issue | After switching Render MCP/CLI to the `IDTS_GSUSAP01` workspace, `render blueprints validate` failed with `repo is required for git-based services` at `services[0].repo`. | The first Render Blueprint defined the service branch and commands but omitted the Git repository URL required by Render CLI validation for Git-backed services. | Fixed in follow-up branch `chore/idts-44-render-blueprint-repo-donhv` by adding `repo: https://github.com/DarwinDO/IDTS-SAP01` to the web service. | Fresh `render blueprints validate` passed with service `idts-sap01-qa` and database `idts-sap01-qa-db`; PR/merge the fix before creating Render resources. |
| Security/configuration issue | The first Render startup log printed the PostgreSQL connection URL, including the generated database password. | CAP `cds-serve all` logs connected service credentials at `info` level, and the current CAP redaction did not mask a database URL string. | Temporary service and database were deleted immediately; replacement resources were created with a new PostgreSQL credential and `cds_log_levels={"cds.serve":"warn"}`. | Add the log-level env var to `render.yaml`, merge it to `dev`, and verify replacement Render logs no longer contain `postgresql://`. |
| Security/configuration issue | The replacement Render service still logged the PostgreSQL connection URL even with the Render env var `cds_log_levels={"cds.serve":"warn"}`. | The env-only mitigation did not suppress the `cds.serve` logger in the Render runtime. | Under fix by adding CAP log-level config directly in `package.json`: `cds.log.levels["cds.serve"]="warn"`. | Rotate the Render DB/service again after this config lands, then verify logs with a redacted/count-only check. |
| Deployment/runtime issue | The first public smoke against the replacement Render service returned HTTP 404 for `/bug-management-ui/webapp/login.html`. | The Render build command ran `npm ci` while `NODE_ENV=production` was set, so npm likely omitted devDependency `cds-plugin-ui5`, which is needed to serve the Fiori/UI5 preview app from CAP in this QA deployment shape. | Under fix in branch `chore/idts-44-render-log-redaction-donhv` by changing the Render QA build command to `npm ci --include=dev`. | Merge the build-command fix, update the live Render service build command, redeploy, then rerun login-page smoke. |
| Deployment/health-check issue | Replacement Render deploy remained `update_in_progress` without error logs, and public requests timed out. | Health check path was `/`, but CAP does not serve a root route for this app; Render likely could not mark the deploy healthy. | Under fix in branch `chore/idts-44-render-log-redaction-donhv` by changing health check to `/odata/v4/auth/$metadata`. | Update live Render service health check, redeploy, then verify deploy reaches `live`. |

## 2026-07-02 - IDTS-44 Render QA account activation

English:

| Classification | Symptom / work | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Deployment verification | The four seeded QA profiles existed in Render PostgreSQL but could not sign in because they had no password hashes. | Seed data intentionally contains profiles only; plaintext credentials are never committed. | Fixed by using the secret-safe password helper to write hashes for DonHV, SangVN, DatDT, and NhanT. A temporary PostgreSQL IP allowlist entry was removed immediately afterward. | Fresh public verification: 4/4 logins passed, wrong password returned HTTP 401, authenticated BugService OData returned HTTP 200, PostgreSQL remained available, and the IP allowlist count returned to 0. Next: verify email and S3 flows in the shared QA environment. |

Allowlist IP PostgreSQL tam thoi da duoc xoa ngay sau do. | Xac minh public moi: 4/4 login pass, password sai tra HTTP 401, BugService OData co auth tra HTTP 200, PostgreSQL van available, va so entry allowlist IP tro ve 0. Tiep theo: verify luong email va S3 tren shared QA. |

## 2026-07-02 - IDTS-48 shared-QA Brevo API closure

English:

| Classification | Symptom / work | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Deployment verification | PR #58 hardening needed to be merged and proven on the actual Render service before IDTS-48 could close. | Shared-QA acceptance requires evidence from the deployed `dev` commit, not only local tests or a mergeable PR. | Completed. PR #58 was merged at `bcf43b2`; Render deploy `dep-d931rb6rnols73851g1g` reached `live`. | Auth metadata and login page returned 200; wrong login and anonymous protected OData returned 401; authenticated OData returned 200. |
| Email delivery verification | The prior Render-to-Brevo SMTP path timed out, so a real provider delivery was still required after switching to the Transactional API. | Render outbound SMTP reachability was unreliable; HTTPS API transport avoids that network path while preserving the database outbox. | Fixed through IDTS-48. A new assignment notification reached `SENT` on the first attempt and recorded `sentAt` plus a provider message id. | Post-deploy email log reported `brevo-api: sent=1, failed=0`; no new provider error was found. Jira IDTS-48 moved to Done. |
| Tooling issue | `gh pr merge 58 --squash --delete-branch` ended with `fatal: 'dev' is already used by worktree at 'E:/IDTS-SAP01'`. | GitHub CLI completed the remote merge, then local branch cleanup conflicted with the root worktree already using `dev`. | No product or remote merge failure. Remote PR state and merge commit were checked separately and confirmed. | Keep using a clean worktree for PM evidence; avoid local branch deletion through `gh pr merge` while root `dev` is attached. |
| Deployment integration issue | Render auto-deploy did not immediately create a deploy for the merged PR #58 commit. | The GitHub/Render auto-deploy integration remains intermittent. | Worked around by explicitly deploying exact commit `bcf43b2` with Render CLI. | Deploy reached `live`; keep auto-deploy reliability as an IDTS-44 follow-up rather than an application defect. |
| Tooling warning | PR #59 `qa-depth-gate` passed, but GitHub warned that Node.js 20 is deprecated for `actions/checkout@v4` and `actions/setup-node@v4`, so the runner forced those actions onto Node.js 24. | GitHub-hosted runner runtime policy has moved beyond the action runtime declared by the current action versions. | Open, non-blocking; no application test failed. | Track a narrow CI maintenance follow-up to upgrade the affected action versions when supported; do not mix it into the documentation-only acceptance PR. |
| Tooling issue | The first GitHub API merge attempt for PR #59 returned HTTP 422 because the `sha` parameter was not received as one 40-character hexadecimal value. | PowerShell argument binding split or reshaped the `-f sha=$pr.headRefOid` expression before `gh api` sent it. | No merge or repository state change occurred. The retry will use an explicitly interpolated `--raw-field` value after validating its length and format locally. | Rerun the PR gate after this status update, then merge only when the exact head SHA and successful checks are confirmed. |

PR #58 merge tai `bcf43b2`; Render deploy `dep-d931rb6rnols73851g1g` dat `live`. | Auth metadata va login page tra 200; login sai va protected OData anonymous tra 401; OData co auth tra 200. |
| Xac minh email delivery | Duong SMTP Render-to-Brevo truoc do timeout, nen van can mot delivery provider that sau khi doi sang Transactional API. | Outbound SMTP tu Render khong on dinh; transport HTTPS API tranh duong mang do nhung van giu database outbox. | Da fix qua IDTS-48. Notification assign moi dat `SENT` ngay lan dau, co `sentAt` va provider message id. | Log email sau deploy ghi `brevo-api: sent=1, failed=0`; khong co provider error moi. Jira IDTS-48 da chuyen Done. |
| Loi tooling | `gh pr merge 58 --squash --delete-branch` ket thuc voi `fatal: 'dev' is already used by worktree at 'E:/IDTS-SAP01'`. | GitHub CLI da merge remote, sau do cleanup branch local bi conflict vi root worktree dang dung `dev`. | Khong phai loi san pham hay loi merge remote. Da kiem tra rieng trang thai PR va merge commit. | Tiep tuc dung clean worktree cho PM evidence; khong de `gh pr merge` xoa branch local khi root `dev` dang attached. |

## 2026-07-02 - IDTS-44 shared QA acceptance implementation

English:

| Classification | Symptom | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Tooling/context issue | A read command executed from the root worktree could not find `render.yaml`, although the file exists on `origin/dev` and in the IDTS-44 worktree. | The root `dev` worktree had not yet incorporated the latest deployment commits when the read was attempted. | No product fix required; implementation continues from the clean IDTS-44 worktree based on current `origin/dev`. | Verify all deployment files from `E:/IDTS-SAP01-worktrees/idts-44-render`. |
| Tooling issue | Render MCP read-only PostgreSQL query failed with `FATAL: SSL/TLS required` and unexpected EOF. | The MCP database connector did not negotiate the SSL requirement of this Render PostgreSQL instance. | Open as a connector limitation; do not classify as a product or database defect. | Use the proven Render CLI + temporary allowlist + Node `pg` SSL path, then clear the allowlist in `finally`. |
| Deployment integration issue | Merging PR #53 into `dev` did not create a new Render deploy even though the service reports `autoDeploy=yes` and branch `dev`. | The Render service's GitHub auto-deploy webhook/integration is not firing; exact provider-side cause is still under investigation. | Open; use an explicit Render CLI deploy of the merged commit so acceptance can continue, then verify/fix the Git integration separately. | Trigger commit `cec3958` with `render deploys create --commit ... --wait`; record deploy status and keep auto-deploy repair as an IDTS-44 follow-up if it still does not fire. |
| Dependency security issue | The clean Render build reports 20 npm vulnerabilities with the current install: 13 moderate and 7 high. | Existing dependency tree contains vulnerable transitive/direct packages; the build task does not establish whether safe non-breaking upgrades are available. | Open and out of scope for the deployment acceptance; do not run broad `npm audit fix --force`. | Create or update a dedicated dependency-security Jira item with sanitized `npm audit` evidence and review upgrades separately. |
| Deployment data/tooling issue | First cloud identity update failed with `operator does not exist: character varying = uuid`. | CAP stores UUID-shaped keys as PostgreSQL character fields in this deployed schema, while the helper compared them to an explicit `uuid[]` parameter. | Safely rolled back; no partial email update. Fix helper casts to `text[]` and add a regression assertion. | Rerun focused helper test, secret scan and cloud update; confirm old aliases still work before retry and allowlist count is 0. |
| Environment configuration blocker | Enabling recipient-real Brevo SMTP on Render produced `missing fields: host, username, password, fromAddress`. | The required Brevo secret variables are not configured on the current Render web service, even though they may exist in DonHV's local private config. | Email was immediately switched back to disabled so bug workflows remain safe and deliveries can be `SKIPPED`. | DonHV must enter the four Brevo values directly in Render Dashboard; then rerun the recipient routing and failure/retry acceptance without sharing secrets. |
| Environment configuration blocker | Shared-QA attachment flow passed comment/history, draft edit, and attachment metadata creation, but stream upload returned HTTP 500. | Render log confirms `SAP Object Store instance is not bound`; the AWS S3 bucket/region/access-key/secret binding is absent from the current service. | Open; the failed QA draft was deleted successfully with HTTP 204, so no draft metadata was left behind. | DonHV must enter the four S3 values directly in Render Dashboard; then rerun upload/activate/download/redeploy/download/delete acceptance. |
| Deployment verification | Corrected identity update completed for all four stable users, revoked five old sessions, and preserved role/password state. Clean deploys of the merged `dev` commits reached `live`. | Stable-UUID helper now matches CAP PostgreSQL character keys with `text[]`; secrets remain process-only. | Verified. Four new login identities return 200, old alias returns 401, protected OData returns 200, and one Bearer session remained valid across redeploy. | Finish Brevo/S3 acceptance after Dashboard secrets are entered; keep IDTS-44 In Progress. Jira IDTS-45 tracks DB expiry and IDTS-46 tracks dependency security. |
| Tooling issue | First attempt to read the configured Render API bearer for a secret-safe local-to-Render transfer reported that the bearer was not found. | The PowerShell regex assumed `Authorization` started a TOML line, but Codex stores it inline inside `http_headers`. | Under fix; no credential or Render environment value was changed. | Retry with an inline-header regex, output only env response shape/keys, and never print the bearer or secret values. |
| Tooling issue | First batch Render env PUT was rejected with `environment variable keys must consist...`. | The GET response/payload contains an item that must be filtered or reshaped before using the replace-all endpoint. | No partial update occurred because Render rejected validation before applying the payload. | Inspect key names only, filter invalid/empty cursor items, then retry while preserving every existing valid env var. |
| Tooling issue | A Render env transfer retry failed before any API update with `System.Boolean does not contain a method named ToLowerInvariant`. | PowerShell evaluated the private SMTP `secure` value as a Boolean before string conversion. | No Render environment value was changed; rerun with explicit Boolean-to-string normalization. | Verify output prints only key names/counts and no secret values. |
| Tooling/config issue | The next Render env transfer reached the API but failed with `must provide a value or generateValue must be set to true`. | Render's single-env update endpoint rejects empty-string values; the transfer attempted to set an optional blank value such as default test recipient. | Some earlier non-empty env keys may have been updated before the failure; retry will skip optional blank values and use `testMode=false` so no default test recipient is required. | Re-query key names only after retry and trigger a fresh deploy before acceptance testing. |
| Tooling/deployment issue | `render deploys create srv-d92jk67aqgkc739h6ah0 --wait -o json` failed with interactive prompt EOF. | Render CLI requires an explicit confirmation flag or API path when run non-interactively. | No deploy was created by that failed command. | Use the non-interactive deploy path and verify deploy status reaches `live`. |
| Tooling/deployment issue | Non-interactive Render deploy with `--confirm --wait` exceeded the 15-minute local command timeout. | The CLI wait window was longer than the command timeout; deploy status must be checked separately before deciding whether it failed. | Under investigation; do not classify as product failure until Render deploy status/logs are read. | Query latest deploy status and logs, then either continue acceptance if `live` or triage the deploy failure. |
| Tooling issue | `render deploys list srv-d92jk67aqgkc739h6ah0 --limit 5 -o json` failed with `unknown flag: --limit`. | Render CLI v2.21.0 does not support a limit flag for this subcommand. | No service state changed; rerun without `--limit` and filter locally. | Use documented CLI flags only for remaining deploy checks. |
| Tooling issue | First sanitized Render log read failed in PowerShell with `Unexpected token '??'`. | The local Windows PowerShell version does not support the null-coalescing operator used in the ad-hoc log sanitizer. | No Render read was completed and no service state changed. | Rerun the sanitizer with Windows PowerShell-compatible conditional logic. |
| Tooling/logging issue | The second sanitized Render log read failed at `ConvertFrom-Json` because Render CLI returned newline-adjacent JSON objects instead of a single JSON array. | The CLI log output shape differs from the deploy list output shape; the ad-hoc parser assumed array JSON. | The log output was still useful: it showed the failed deploy timed out with no detected port, while the live instance now recognizes AWS S3 config but still reports email disabled. | Use targeted log text filters and Render env key inspection instead of parsing full log streams. |
| Test-harness issue | First shared-QA S3 smoke wrapper stopped before the attachment script with `No bearer token captured`. | The wrapper did not expose enough sanitized diagnostics to distinguish DB query, login request, or output capture failure. | No attachment upload was attempted; PostgreSQL allowlist cleanup ran in `finally`. | Verify allowlist is empty, then rerun a diagnostic that reports only counts/status codes and never prints email, token, or DB URL. |
| Environment/test-harness issue | Second shared-QA S3 smoke wrapper still stopped before the attachment script; Node `pg` reported `Connection terminated unexpectedly`. | External PostgreSQL access through the temporary allowlist was unstable or not ready immediately after allowlist update. | No attachment upload was attempted; cleanup still ran in `finally`. | Rerun DB/token bootstrap with a short wait and retry loop, then run the attachment script only after token capture succeeds. |
| Product/environment blocker | Shared-QA S3 smoke with a valid Bearer token still failed at `SC-CA-04 Upload draft attachment stream` with HTTP 500. | Under investigation; the app now recognizes `aws-s3`, so the earlier missing-binding blocker changed into a provider/runtime upload failure. | Open. Comment/history/draft metadata passed; binary upload failed and draft cleanup is required. | Read targeted Render attachment logs, clean the failed draft, and determine whether the issue is S3 credential/bucket policy, endpoint/provider config, or plugin runtime behavior. |
| Tooling issue | Local CAP env mapping test for dotted env var names failed with PowerShell parser errors using `$env:'key.with.dots'`. | PowerShell cannot assign dotted environment variable names with that syntax. | No environment or source state changed. | Rerun using `Set-Item Env:\<name> <value>` for dotted env var keys. |
| Deployment configuration issue | Render S3 upload kept failing until object-store credentials were provided as a JSON env object through `cds_requires_objectStore_credentials`. | CAP underscore env parsing turned `access_key_id` into nested `access.key.id`, so the plugin could see bucket/region but not `access_key_id` or `secret_access_key`. | Fixed for shared QA; deploy `dep-d92t3dkvikkc73b3mbb0` and rerun attachment smoke passed upload, activation, download, and history. | Keep Render Dashboard/env docs updated to prefer JSON object or dotted env keys for credential fields that contain underscores. |
| SMTP environment issue | Brevo-enabled workflow created an email delivery row, but the worker changed it to `FAILED` with `ETIMEDOUT` / `SMTP connection timed out`. | Render could not complete the SMTP connection on the configured port 587, or the Brevo SMTP relay is not reachable/active from Render on that port. | Open; bug workflow committed successfully, proving SMTP failure does not rollback the business action. | Try Brevo port 2525 with `secure=false` per Brevo guidance for blocked 587, then rerun one delivery smoke. |
| SMTP environment issue | Retrying the same Brevo delivery after switching Render to port 2525 still ended with `FAILED`, `attemptCount=3`, and sanitized `ETIMEDOUT`; local Nodemailer `verify()` to Brevo passed from DonHV's machine. | The credential and Brevo account are likely valid, but the Render runtime cannot complete outbound SMTP to Brevo on the tested ports. | Open; IDTS-44 must stay In Progress for real email delivery. Email outbox creation, retry bookkeeping, sanitized error storage, and failure-does-not-rollback behavior are verified. | Decide whether to investigate Render/Brevo network reachability, contact provider support, switch to Brevo HTTP API, or use another provider for shared QA. |
| Tooling issue | After PR #55 was merged, `gh pr merge --delete-branch` could not complete local cleanup because branch `dev` is already checked out by the main worktree at `E:/IDTS-SAP01`. | Git worktree isolation prevents this secondary IDTS-44 worktree from checking out/deleting a branch currently owned by another worktree. | Non-product issue; PR #55 was merged successfully and `origin/dev` advanced to merge commit `b2803c3`. | Leave local cleanup to a separate branch/worktree-safe command; do not treat this as a Render or IDTS product defect. |

| Loi tooling | Sau khi PR #55 merge, `gh pr merge --delete-branch` khong cleanup local duoc vi branch `dev` dang duoc worktree chinh tai `E:/IDTS-SAP01` su dung. | Git worktree khong cho secondary worktree checkout/delete branch dang thuoc worktree khac. | Khong phai loi product; PR #55 merge thanh cong va `origin/dev` da len merge commit `b2803c3`. | Neu can cleanup branch thi dung lenh tach rieng an toan cho worktree; khong xem day la loi Render hay IDTS. |

- DonHV cập nhật file này cho quyết định của leader, việc BA/PM, deliverable SAP490, tổng hợp hằng tuần và hỗ trợ cross-workstream.
- Khi DonHV hỗ trợ task của thành viên khác, ghi phần hỗ trợ ở đây và nêu rõ file member/task bị ảnh hưởng.

## 2026-07-02 - IDTS-48 replyTo config hardening

English:

| Classification | Symptom / work | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Product hardening | Implemented config validation so optional email `replyTo` must be a plain safe email address and cannot use placeholder/display syntax such as `<optional-reply-to@example.test>`. | The shared-QA Brevo API failure showed that the old basic email regex allowed angle brackets, which Brevo rejects in `replyTo.email`. | Fixed in `srv/email/config.js`, focused tests, `.cdsrc-private.example.json`, and knowledge mirror. | Fresh verification passed: `node --check`, `npm run qa:email-outbox:programmatic`, `npm run qa:email-brevo-api:integration`, `npx cds compile srv --to edmx -s all`, `npm run qa:secret-scan`, `npx ai-devkit@latest lint --json`, and `git diff --check`. |
| Tooling/process issue | The first focused test run in the new worktree failed with `Cannot find module '@sap/cds'`. | The new isolated worktree had no `node_modules` yet. | Fixed by running `npm ci --include=dev` in the worktree. | Dependency install completed, but it reported the existing known 20 vulnerabilities already tracked separately; no dependency versions were changed. |

Retry with URL-encoded `%24select` and `%24filter`. | Count matching QA recipients with `SENT`, `sentAt`, and provider-reference evidence without printing email addresses. |

| Product defect candidate | A controlled PM-authenticated bug draft created through shared-QA OData failed activation with HTTP 400 `Reporter is required` targeting `reporter_ID`. | Under investigation. The custom-auth backend is expected to derive the reporter from the authenticated IDTS user; either draft create/activation mapping is missing or the direct harness omitted a contract field that the UI normally supplies. | Open; IDTS-44 cannot be closed until this is classified and either fixed or proven to be harness misuse. The controlled failed draft remains for cleanup. | Inspect `bug-write` request-user mapping and the live draft payload; search Jira for an existing matching defect before creating a new one. |

| Product defect | Source review confirmed draft `NEW` only enforces create permission, while reporter derivation occurs in active `CREATE`; custom `SAVE` validates the draft first and rejects missing reporter. | System-managed reporter initialization is registered too late for draft activation. | Open as Jira IDTS-49 and linked as blocking IDTS-44. Controlled failed drafts were deleted. | Implement a draft-aware, idempotent initialization path, add regression coverage, deploy, and rerun DonHV recipient routing before closure. |
| Deployment verification | Final IDTS-44 audit refreshed auth/OData, Render, Brevo, and S3 evidence. | Closure required current-state proof rather than relying only on previous session results. | PASS except IDTS-49. Current `dev` redeploy reached `live`; auth/OData status matrix passed; NhanT gained a new `SENT`; S3 survived redeploy with matching SHA-256 and cleanup returned 204/404. | Await approval of the IDTS-49 fix plan; keep IDTS-44 In Progress. |

## 2026-07-02 - IDTS-49 draft reporter fix

English:

| Classification | Symptom / work | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Dependency security issue | `npm ci --include=dev` completed in the clean IDTS-49 worktree but reported 20 vulnerabilities: 13 moderate and 7 high, plus deprecation warnings in transitive packages. | Existing repository dependency tree; IDTS-46 already tracks targeted remediation. | Open outside IDTS-49. No dependency version or lockfile change is planned in this draft bug fix. | Do not run broad or forced audit fixes; proceed with focused regression coverage. |
| Test-harness issue | The first in-memory `NEW` draft probe reproduced `reporter_ID = null`, then CAP's background draft-cleanup job logged missing table `DRAFT_DraftAdministrativeData` after service disconnect. | The ad-hoc in-memory deployment did not provision the draft administrative table expected by the background cleanup timer. | The reporter regression signal is valid and the probe exited 0, but the background log is noisy. | Build the committed focused test so it controls/disables the background cleanup path or deploys the complete draft model; do not misclassify this as the IDTS-49 product defect. |
| Test-harness issue | The first post-fix focused test process exited 0 after only 2 of 6 planned assertions. | Multiple direct `cds.tx(req)` calls outside service dispatch produced an unstable request context; Node exited while the next direct helper promise had no active runtime handle. | Test result is rejected as incomplete despite exit 0. Refactor the small helper boundary to accept the already resolved actor, then test deterministic pure initialization plus the permission resolver separately. | Add an explicit completion-count guard so partial execution cannot pass again. |
| Product fix | Implemented draft-aware reporter initialization for IDTS-49. Draft `NEW` now uses the actor returned by create permission, and draft `SAVE` fills reporter before required-field validation for older incomplete drafts. | Root cause was reporter derivation happening only in active `CREATE`, after draft `SAVE` validation had already rejected missing `reporter_ID`. | Locally fixed and verified; pending PR merge, Render deploy, and shared-QA DonHV reporter-routing smoke before closing IDTS-49/44. | Fresh evidence passed: `node --check`, `npm run qa:draft-reporter:programmatic` 10/10, `npx cds compile srv --to edmx -s all`, `npm run qa:auth:programmatic` 23/23, `npm run qa:email-outbox:programmatic`, `npm run qa:depth:self-test` 6/6, `node scripts/qa/test-idts41-code-list-validation.js` 18/18, `npm run qa:secret-scan`, `npx ai-devkit@latest lint --json`, and `git diff --check`. |
| PM/Jira closure | Closed IDTS-49 and IDTS-44 after merge/deploy/shared-QA verification. | The only IDTS-44 blocker was IDTS-49; after PR #61 and Render deploy, the missing reporter-routing scenario passed. | Done. Jira IDTS-49 and IDTS-44 moved to Done with sanitized evidence comments. | PR #61 merged; Render deploy `dep-d93420m7r5hc73a45dvg` live; shared-QA smoke passed auth/OData, PM draft activation without client reporter, Developer request-more-information, and reporter delivery `SENT` attempt 1; Render log check had no new `Reporter is required` and Brevo API logged `sent=2, failed=0`. |

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Loi security dependency | `npm ci --include=dev` hoan tat trong clean worktree IDTS-49 nhung bao 20 vulnerabilities: 13 moderate va 7 high, kem warning deprecated o transitive package. | Dependency tree hien co cua repo; IDTS-46 da track remediation co muc tieu. | Con mo ngoai pham vi IDTS-49. Khong doi dependency version hay lockfile trong bug fix draft nay. | Khong chay audit fix rong/force; tiep tuc focused regression coverage. |
| Loi test-harness | Probe draft `NEW` in-memory dau reproduce `reporter_ID = null`, sau do background draft-cleanup cua CAP log thieu table `DRAFT_DraftAdministrativeData` sau disconnect. | Ad-hoc in-memory deployment chua provision draft administrative table ma cleanup timer mong doi. | Tin hieu regression reporter hop le va probe exit 0, nhung background log bi nhieu. | Xay focused test chinh thuc de control/disable cleanup path hoac deploy day du draft model; khong ghi nham day la product defect IDTS-49. |
| Loi test-harness | Focused test sau fix lan dau exit 0 sau khi chi chay 2/6 assertion du kien. | Nhieu lan goi truc tiep `cds.tx(req)` ngoai service dispatch tao request context khong on dinh; Node thoat khi helper promise tiep theo khong co runtime handle. | Khong chap nhan ket qua test du exit 0. Refactor boundary nho de helper nhan actor da resolve, sau do test deterministic initialization va permission resolver rieng. | Them completion-count guard de partial execution khong the pass lai. |
| Fix product | Da implement reporter initialization co nhan thuc draft cho IDTS-49. Draft `NEW` dung actor do create permission tra ve, va draft `SAVE` dien reporter truoc required-field validation cho draft cu bi thieu. | Root cause la reporter chi duoc derive o active `CREATE`, trong khi draft `SAVE` validation da reject thieu `reporter_ID` truoc do. | Da fix va verify local; con cho PR merge, deploy Render, va shared-QA smoke routing ve reporter DonHV truoc khi dong IDTS-49/44. | Evidence moi da pass: `node --check`, `npm run qa:draft-reporter:programmatic` 10/10, `npx cds compile srv --to edmx -s all`, `npm run qa:auth:programmatic` 23/23, `npm run qa:email-outbox:programmatic`, `npm run qa:depth:self-test` 6/6, `node scripts/qa/test-idts41-code-list-validation.js` 18/18, `npm run qa:secret-scan`, `npx ai-devkit@latest lint --json`, va `git diff --check`. |

Jira IDTS-49 va IDTS-44 da chuyen Done voi evidence comment da sanitize. | PR #61 da merge; Render deploy `dep-d93420m7r5hc73a45dvg` live; shared-QA smoke pass auth/OData, PM draft activation khong can client reporter, Developer request-more-information, va reporter delivery `SENT` attempt 1; Render log khong co `Reporter is required` moi va Brevo API ghi `sent=2, failed=0`. |

JQL also confirms Sprint 3 has zero active non-Done issues. |

A standalone Playwright session also reached Atlassian login instead of the already-authenticated board. | Sprint creation is a Jira Agile board operation, and the non-Chrome browser session did not share DonHV's logged-in Chrome profile. | Resolved for this session by using the Chrome plugin with DonHV's existing authenticated Jira tab, then using the discovered sprint id with Jira issue edit API. | Chrome UI verified `IDTS Sprint 4` on board `34` for `4 Jul - 18 Jul`; Jira API moved tasks by `customfield_10020 = 70`; follow-up JQL verified the result. |

| Process hardening | Added Jira task naming, due-date, dependency-link, bug-report, QA-evidence, and knowledge-mirror rules to `AGENTS.md`. | Previous process rules covered bug logging and PR QA, but did not explicitly force Jira summaries/descriptions/links to be complete. | Done in repo docs. | Verify with `rg`, `git diff --check`, and AI DevKit lint. |

## 2026-07-07 - IDTS-63 AI guardrail discovery

English:

| Classification | Symptom / work | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Tooling issue | The first skill read attempted `C:\\Users\\LapHub\\.agents\\skills`, which does not exist in this environment. | The active IDTS skills are repository-local under `E:\\IDTS-SAP01\\.agents\\skills`; the initial lookup used the wrong skill root. | Fixed in-session. No repository or product state was affected. | Re-read `karpathy-guidelines` and `product-discovery` from the repository-local paths before continuing IDTS-63. |
| Tooling issue | A focused PowerShell document-range read failed before reading files because `$f:$s` was parsed as an invalid variable reference. | PowerShell requires braces when a variable is immediately followed by `:` in an interpolated string. | Fixed in-session. No file was changed by the failed command. | Retry with `${f}:${s}` and continue the same read-only inspection. |
| Git process issue | The first commit continued even though `git diff --cached --check` reported trailing whitespace and final blank lines. | The PowerShell commands were separated with `;`, so the failed check did not stop the following commit command. | Being corrected before push; no remote state was changed. | Remove every finding, rerun the staged check with an explicit stop condition, and amend the current commit. |
| Chrome tooling issue | Chrome reached the IDTS-63 Jira page, but the first DOM snapshot failed inside the connected extension before any upload interaction. | The connected Chrome surface did not provide the incremental ARIA snapshot function expected by that Playwright snapshot path. | Under recovery; no file or comment was submitted through Chrome. | Reacquire a fresh Chrome browser/tab per plugin guidance and use an advertised DOM/CUA alternative if the Playwright snapshot path remains unavailable. |
| Chrome upload tooling issue | Jira exposed one hidden file input, but the direct file-chooser click timed out; the Chrome troubleshooting path then required additional extension file-URL access. | Jira's upload input is hidden behind its UI control, and the current Chrome extension permission is insufficient for this upload path. | Closed as a tooling handoff by DonHV's decision; no file was uploaded by the agent. | Keep evidence in the repository and tell DonHV exactly which sanitized file to attach manually after completion. |
| Chrome session cleanup issue | The final Chrome cleanup call could not reuse the previously claimed Jira tab binding after the browser runtime was reacquired. | Reinitializing the Chrome runtime cleared the prior tab variable from the active browser-control context. | No Jira state or local file was affected. Browser work was stopped and will not be retried for this task. | Continue through Jira MCP/GitHub CLI and leave manual attachment to DonHV. |

## 2026-07-07 - IDTS-46 npm dependency security review

English:

| Classification | Symptom / work | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Dependency security finding | Fresh audit of the latest `dev` lockfile reports 6 moderate runtime findings and 20 full-tree findings (13 moderate, 7 high, 0 critical). Runtime findings enter through `@cap-js/attachments` and its installed but provider-specific Google Cloud dependency chain; high findings are currently in UI5/CDS development tooling. | Current direct and transitive package versions in `package-lock.json`; reachability and compatible fixes still require package-path analysis. | IDTS-46 moved to In Progress. No dependency has been changed and no force-fix was run. | Install the exact lockfile in the isolated worktree, trace every high/runtime path, then apply only compatible targeted updates or document evidence-backed residual risk. |
| Tooling/environment issue | `npm explain` returned `No dependencies found` for the audited packages in the fresh worktree. | The worktree had no `node_modules`; `npm audit` can inspect the lockfile, but `npm explain` needs the installed dependency tree. | Identified; not a product defect. | Run `npm ci --include=dev` in the isolated worktree, then repeat dependency-path inspection. |
| Tooling/command behavior | `npm outdated --json` returned exit code 1 while producing a valid outdated-package report. | npm intentionally exits nonzero when outdated dependencies are found; the command did not fail to collect data. | Classified as expected diagnostic behavior, not a product defect. | Use the report to update only versions allowed by current compatibility ranges, then rerun audit and regressions. |

| Phân loại | Triệu chứng / công việc | Nguyên nhân | Trạng thái xử lý | Xác minh / bước tiếp theo |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Phát hiện security dependency | Audit mới trên lockfile `dev` báo 6 finding moderate ở runtime và 20 finding toàn cây (13 moderate, 7 high, 0 critical). Finding runtime đi qua `@cap-js/attachments` và chuỗi Google Cloud theo provider đã được cài; finding high hiện nằm trong tooling UI5/CDS dùng khi phát triển. | Phiên bản direct/transitive hiện có trong `package-lock.json`; vẫn cần phân tích dependency path, reachability và bản fix tương thích. | IDTS-46 đã chuyển In Progress. Chưa đổi dependency và không chạy force-fix. | Cài đúng lockfile trong worktree cô lập, trace toàn bộ path high/runtime, rồi chỉ update có mục tiêu hoặc ghi residual risk có evidence. |
| Lỗi tooling/môi trường | `npm explain` trả `No dependencies found` cho các package đang audit trong worktree mới. | Worktree chưa có `node_modules`; `npm audit` đọc được lockfile nhưng `npm explain` cần cây dependency đã cài. | Đã xác định; không phải product defect. | Chạy `npm ci --include=dev` trong worktree cô lập rồi kiểm tra lại dependency path. |
| Hành vi command/tooling | `npm outdated --json` trả exit code 1 dù đã xuất báo cáo package outdated hợp lệ. | npm chủ động trả mã khác 0 khi tìm thấy dependency outdated; command không thất bại trong việc thu thập dữ liệu. | Đã phân loại là hành vi diagnostic dự kiến, không phải product defect. | Dùng báo cáo để update đúng version nằm trong compatibility range, sau đó audit và regression lại. |

## 2026-07-07 - IDTS-45 Docker PostgreSQL client fallback

English:

| Classification | Symptom / work | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Environment/tooling blocker | Host `pg_dump` and `pg_restore` are still missing from PATH. | PostgreSQL client tools are not installed on Windows. | Avoided installing full PostgreSQL by using Docker Desktop with image `postgres:15` as a client-tool fallback. | `docker run --rm postgres:15 pg_dump --version` and `pg_restore --version` both returned PostgreSQL 15.18. |
| Environment/tooling issue | Docker CLI existed but Docker Desktop daemon was not running at first; `docker info` failed with missing `dockerDesktopLinuxEngine` pipe. | Docker Desktop was installed but not started in the current Windows session. | Started Docker Desktop in the background and verified daemon version 28.5.1. Not a product defect. | Use Docker fallback only when Docker Desktop is running. |
| DevOps continuity work | Backup and restore helpers previously required host PostgreSQL client tools. | This made IDTS-45 harder on Windows machines without PostgreSQL installed. | Updated helpers to prefer host tools when present and fall back to `postgres:15` Docker clients without printing secret values. | Backup preflight with dummy URL now reports `pg_dump available via docker`; restore inspect with an invalid dummy dump reports `pg_restore available via docker` then fails correctly because the file is not a valid archive. |

Vietnamese:

| Phan loai | Trieu chung / cong viec | Nguyen nhan | Trang thai xu ly | Verify / buoc tiep theo |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Blocker moi truong/tooling | Host `pg_dump` va `pg_restore` van chua co trong PATH. | May Windows chua cai PostgreSQL client tools. | Khong cai full PostgreSQL; dung Docker Desktop voi image `postgres:15` lam fallback client tools. | `docker run --rm postgres:15 pg_dump --version` va `pg_restore --version` deu tra PostgreSQL 15.18. |
| Loi moi truong/tooling | Docker CLI co san nhung Docker Desktop daemon ban dau chua chay; `docker info` fail vi thieu pipe `dockerDesktopLinuxEngine`. | Docker Desktop da cai nhung chua start trong Windows session hien tai. | Da start Docker Desktop o nen va verify daemon version 28.5.1. Khong phai product defect. | Chi dung Docker fallback khi Docker Desktop dang chay. |
| DevOps continuity | Backup va restore helpers truoc do bat buoc co PostgreSQL client tools tren host. | Dieu nay lam IDTS-45 kho chay hon tren may Windows chua cai PostgreSQL. | Da update helpers: uu tien host tools neu co, fallback sang Docker `postgres:15` neu Docker dang chay, khong in secret values. | Backup preflight voi dummy URL bao `pg_dump available via docker`; restore inspect voi dummy dump invalid bao `pg_restore available via docker` roi fail dung vi file khong phai archive hop le. |

| Tooling issue | An initial source inspection attempted to read `srv/bug-service.js`, which does not exist. | The runtime service entry is `srv/service.js`; `srv/bug-service/` is a module directory, not a sibling `bug-service.js` file. | Corrected the inspected path to `srv/service.js`. No product code was affected. | Confirmed the runtime handler registration in `srv/service.js`. |
| Environment setup issue | The first module load check failed with `Cannot find module '@sap/cds'`. | The fresh IDTS-66 worktree had no `node_modules`. | Ran `npm ci --include=dev`; this is not a product defect. | Rerun module load and focused verification. |

| Model/compiler warning | CAP compile passed but repeated the existing warning that `NonUpdateableProperties` is not a known property of `@Capabilities.UpdateRestrictions` on `BugService.Bugs_attachments`. | Existing attachment annotation compatibility warning; IDTS-66 does not change attachment annotations. | Logged as an existing non-blocking model warning, not an IDTS-66 product defect. | Keep the warning under attachment/model cleanup; IDTS-66 compile remains successful. |

No product or test code was affected. | Continue with the existing direct CAP service test style used by this repository. |
| Test-harness issue | An attempted authorization assertion called the service directly with `tx.send()` and expected the protocol-level `@requires` guard to reject it, but the direct in-process dispatch bypassed that HTTP authorization path. | The test shape was wrong for a service-level authorization annotation; it did not represent an OData request through the custom-auth middleware. | Removed the misleading direct authorization assertion. The service remains protected by `@(requires: 'authenticated-user')`, and existing auth regression proves protected HTTP/OData behavior. | Keep IDTS-66 focused on action behavior; use existing auth HTTP/browser coverage for protocol authorization evidence. |
| Tooling issue | The first GitHub PR creation orchestration failed before invoking GitHub with a JavaScript `Unexpected identifier 'BugService'` parse error. | Markdown backticks inside the orchestration template were interpreted as JavaScript template delimiters. | Retried with a PowerShell here-string piped to `gh pr create --body-file -`; PR #110 was created successfully. No repository or GitHub state was partially created by the failed attempt. | Verify PR #110 body/checks after the amended status commit is pushed. |

PR #113 was squash-merged into `dev` at `d167613`; Jira comments `10428`, `10429`, and `10430` record implementation, CI pass, and closure. | Verification passed: IDTS-64 `26/0`, IDTS-65 `19/0`, IDTS-66 `30/0`, IDTS-67 `22/0`, CAP compile, secret scan, AI DevKit lint, and `git diff --check`. Evidence is in `docs/pm/evidence/idts-67/README.md`. Jira IDTS-67 moved to Done. |
| Environment setup issue | CAP MCP `search_model` initially failed in the fresh worktree with missing `@cap-js/attachments`. | The fresh IDTS-67 worktree had no `node_modules` yet. | Ran `npm ci --include=dev`; CAP MCP search worked after dependencies were installed. Not a product defect. | Continue using fresh worktrees, but install dependencies before CAP model/MCP checks when the workspace has no `node_modules`. |

| Tooling issue | A PowerShell handover read command used `Select-Object -Index 124..150`, which failed because the range was passed as a string. | PowerShell needs an evaluated range expression or `-Skip/-First` for this usage. | Re-ran the read with `Select-Object -Skip 120 -First 35`. No product or documentation content was affected by the failed command. | Continue using `-Skip/-First` for markdown window reads in PowerShell. |
| Tooling issue | CAP compile completed, but the first post-filter command failed because `Select-String -Pattern` received an incorrectly escaped `suggestClassification` argument. | PowerShell quoting was wrong in the verification wrapper, not in CAP compilation. | Re-ran compile and post-filter with `-SimpleMatch 'suggestClassification'`; compile exited 0 and metadata contains the new action. | CAP compile evidence now uses the rerun output. |

| Environment setup issue | The clean IDTS-72/73 QA worktree initially had no installed dependencies. | Git worktrees do not share `node_modules` with the root checkout. | Resolved by running `npm ci --include=dev`; this is not a product defect. | Continue with local SQLite deploy, CAP startup, and fresh browser execution. |
| Dependency security follow-up | `npm ci --include=dev` reported 14 audit findings: 9 moderate and 5 high. | Existing repository dependency baseline; this QA task adds no runtime dependency. | Open outside IDTS-72/73 and already belongs to the dependency-security follow-up. No forced audit fix is included here. | Run the existing secret/security checks and keep this QA change limited to test tooling and evidence. |
| Tooling issue | The first isolated CAP start probe used the non-existent path `node_modules/@sap/cds/bin/cds.js`; port 4004 was also already owned by the older IDTS-73 worktree. | The executable is provided by `@sap/cds-dk`, and the previous browser-smoke server was still running on its original port. | No product or QA data was changed. Started the current branch explicitly with `node_modules/@sap/cds-dk/bin/cds.js` on port 4005. | Auth metadata on port 4005 returned HTTP 200; all new browser runs target this isolated port. |
| Test-harness issue | The first IDTS-72 browser run found the Smart Assign dialog and valid AI review copy, but failed when it assumed the first candidate row must enable Assign. | Candidate ordering can place a busy/on-leave developer first; selecting that row correctly keeps Assign disabled. | No product defect and no assignment mutation occurred.
