| Test-harness issue | The first IDTS-73 full browser run passed create-page visibility, pending-file, unsupported-type, and oversized-file checks, then timed out looking only for a `Save` button. | A brand-new Fiori draft can expose the final action as `Create` rather than `Save`; the harness over-constrained the button label. | No product defect. The controlled draft was cleaned up by the test finalizer. The submit locator is being broadened to the supported `Create`/`Save` labels. | Rerun the full upload, reload, hash, and failure-recovery scenarios. |
| Test-harness issue | The second IDTS-73 run passed the complete happy flow and controlled upload-failure assertions, but the final generic harness check still counted the intentionally injected HTTP 503 and its expected SAP error dialog as unexpected failures. | Expected negative-test signals were not fully dismissed/cleared before the final no-unexpected-error gate. | Product behavior is correct; the run is not accepted as PASS yet. The script is being changed to close the exact expected dialog and clear only the expected 503 console/response records. | Rerun and require the final harness gate to pass with no unrelated browser errors. |
| Test-harness invocation issue | The first attempt to include Render smoke in IDTS-72 acceptance reached auth metadata but login returned 401. | `IDTS_QA_BASE_URL` was still set to the isolated local port 4005 from the browser run, so the command tested local SQLite with the shared-QA credential variables instead of Render. | Not a product/auth defect. No workflow mutation occurred. The rerun will explicitly set the Render base URL rather than relying on inherited shell state. | Run acceptance again with `https://idts-sap01-qa.onrender.com` and accept only fresh shared-QA evidence. |
| Shared-QA data issue | The explicit Render rerun still returned 401 for the private QA email currently stored in the shell, while a secret-safe identity probe confirmed the seeded DonHV account accepts the same password. | Shared QA currently contains the `.example.local` seed identity rather than the previously customized FPT login email, consistent with a later seed/deploy refresh. | QA can continue with the current seeded identity; no password or real email is stored in evidence. Restoring custom FPT identities remains separate environment setup work. | Rerun Render smoke with the verified seed identity and require all AI/API checks to pass. |
| Existing model warning | CAP compile passed but reported that `NonUpdateableProperties` is not a known property of the attachment `@Capabilities.UpdateRestrictions` annotation. | Existing attachment vocabulary compatibility warning; this QA-only branch does not change CDS or Fiori annotations. | Left unchanged and not classified as an IDTS-72/73 regression. | Keep under the existing attachment/model follow-up if it becomes a runtime issue. |
| QA implementation | Added repeatable IDTS-72 acceptance/browser automation and full IDTS-73 create-time attachment browser regression with selected evidence. | IDTS-72 previously depended on separate feature scripts and IDTS-73 only had static/basic browser proof, so neither task had one repeatable final acceptance path. | Implemented and freshly verified. Combined AI acceptance passed 7/7 including Render 25/25; Smart Assign browser passed; IDTS-73 static passed 16 checks and full browser passed pending selection, upload/hash, reload, validation, and safe failure recovery. | Complete final diff review, commit/push, open PR, then attach selected evidence to Jira and close IDTS-72 after CI passes. |
| Tooling cleanup issue | The first attempt to stop the isolated port-4005 CAP process refused to act because its safety check expected the worktree path in the process command line. | Node was launched with a relative CLI path, so Windows recorded only the relative command and port, not the working directory. | No process was stopped by the failed safety check. Cleanup is retried only after confirming the listener command is the expected CAP `serve all --port 4005` process. | Stop the verified PID and confirm port 4005 is no longer listening. |

## 2026-07-09 - IDTS-74 shared-QA deployment follow-up

English:

| Classification | Symptom / work | Root cause | Fix status | Verification / next action |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Deployment/process issue | Render auto-deploy did not pick up the merged IDTS-74 commit even though the service is configured with `autoDeploy=yes`. Shared QA remained on commit `2f600d4`. | The GitHub-to-Render automatic trigger did not fire for this merge; the exact external trigger failure is not yet proven. | Manually triggered Render deploy `dep-d97p0sq8qa3s73f4tagg` for merged commit `d5e9549`; it reached `live`. | Finish shared-QA API/browser smoke, then track the recurring auto-deploy reliability separately if it happens again. |
| Tooling issue | The first secret-safe environment-variable presence probe failed with PowerShell `An empty pipe element is not allowed`. | A pipeline was placed directly after an inline `foreach` block. | No secret was printed and no product state changed. The probe is being rerun using an explicit result variable. | Use the corrected probe before authenticated shared-QA testing. |
| Tooling issue | The first Render polling command timed out after the deploy had already progressed from build to update. | Repeated Render CLI calls inside the PowerShell loop did not return reliably before the shell timeout. | A separate one-shot `render deploys list` call confirmed deploy `dep-d97p0sq8qa3s73f4tagg` is `live` on commit `d5e9549`. | Prefer one-shot status reads over a long CLI polling loop. |
| Shared-QA data issue | The first authenticated IDTS-74 smoke returned `401` for the private QA email currently stored in the shell. | The Render pre-deploy database step refreshed seeded users, so the previously customized login identity no longer matches the current QA row. | No credential was logged. The smoke is being rerun with the known seeded QA identity and the same private password. | Restore a durable cloud-identity setup separately; do not classify this as an IDTS-74 product regression. |
| Tooling issue | `Get-Date -AsUTC` was rejected while preparing the browser evidence timestamp. | The installed Windows PowerShell version does not support the `-AsUTC` parameter. | Browser evidence and product state were unaffected. Use `[DateTime]::UtcNow.ToString("o")` instead. | Record the compatible UTC timestamp in the evidence summary. |
| Shared-QA acceptance | Reran authenticated API and browser checks against the live IDTS-74 deploy. | The deployed commit needed direct evidence rather than relying on local tests or CI alone. | Passed. Render AI smoke passed 25/25; the deployed Object Page exposed `Find Similar Bugs`; the dialog displayed a candidate, status, match, relation, reason, and manual-review guidance; no Save, Assign, transition, or duplicate-link action was performed. | Evidence is stored under `docs/pm/evidence/idts-74/`; DonHV will manually attach selected artifacts to Jira if needed. |
| Tooling issue | The first stale-text `rg` verification failed with an unterminated PowerShell string. | Backticks inside a double-quoted regex were interpreted by PowerShell. | No files or product state changed. Rerun with single-quoted literal patterns. | Require the corrected search, secret scan, and diff check to pass before commit. |

| Tooling issue | OfficeCLI installer completed, but the same PowerShell session could not resolve `officecli` immediately afterward. | The installer adds `%LOCALAPPDATA%\\OfficeCLI` to PATH for new shells only. | Resolved by invoking `C:\\Users\\LapHub\\AppData\\Local\\OfficeCLI\\officecli.exe` directly; version `1.0.135` and help output are available. This is not a product defect. | Add mandatory OfficeCLI routing to `AGENTS.md`, `.agents/rules/`, and SAP490 sync guidance; use OfficeCLI validation for the regenerated DOCX/PPTX artifacts before Drive upload. |
| Tooling documentation mismatch | The installed OfficeCLI v1.0.135 does not expose the documented `load_skill` command. | Its current CLI uses `officecli skills install <skill> <agent>` instead. | Resolved by following the binary's own help output; do not guess undocumented subcommands. This is not a product defect. | Install the `word` and `pptx` skills for Codex, then use format inspection and validation before every Office artifact handoff. |
| Documentation generator defect | OfficeCLI schema validation found generated DOCX table-header shading without required `w:val` on `w:shd`. | The `python-docx` generator set only `w:fill`. | Fix in progress: set `w:val=clear`, rebuild all six DOCX files, then rerun OfficeCLI validation before any rendering or Drive sync. This is not a product defect. | Keep all generated DOCX files local until the corrected validation output is clean. |
| Documentation generator follow-up | Adding `w:val=clear` reduced validation findings but did not resolve the remaining unexpected `w:shd` ordering errors. | The generator appends shading after later cell-property nodes; OpenXML requires schema order. | Under investigation; no DOCX is accepted for review or Drive upload. This is not a product defect. | Inspect one generated `tcPr`, insert shading before later cell properties, rebuild, then rerun OfficeCLI validation for all six files. |
| Documentation generator resolution | All six regenerated BRD/SRS/FRS DOCX files now pass OfficeCLI OpenXML validation. | Header shading is emitted with `w:val=clear` before `w:vAlign`, matching the required cell-property order. | Fixed and verified; this remains a documentation tooling issue, not a product defect. | Continue with Documents/LibreOffice visual rendering before Google Drive upload. |
| Documentation rendering tooling issue | The first Documents render invocation failed with `ModuleNotFoundError: pdf2image`. | The system Python interpreter was used instead of the workspace-bundled document runtime. | Fixed path identified; no DOCX content changed and no file was uploaded. This is not a product defect. | Rerun the same renderer with the bundled Python executable, then inspect every rendered page. |
| Documentation rendering tooling follow-up | The bundled Documents renderer did not fail immediately but exceeded the 180-second batch timeout while rendering six DOCX files. | Rendering all documents in one process is too broad for the current timeout and may be waiting on a large FRS conversion. | Open; no visual-QA result is accepted yet and no Drive upload will occur. This is not a product defect. | Inspect any completed render folders, then render each document separately with bounded timeout and continue from completed output. |
| OfficeCLI resident-file behavior | Rebuilding a DOCX immediately after `officecli refresh` raised `PermissionError` because the OfficeCLI Word resident still held the file handle. | `refresh` intentionally keeps a resident Office process open until `officecli close <file>` is called. | Resolved by closing all six DOCX residents before rebuilding. This is not a product defect. | Always close a refreshed OfficeCLI document before a non-OfficeCLI writer regenerates it; rebuild, refresh, validate, and then inspect native screenshots. |
| SAP490 Diagram Pack distribution | Generated 21 canonical diagram source fragments, 21 SVG review assets, and a 24-slide native Google Slides Diagram Pack; uploaded the 30 source/manifest files and 21 SVG files into the new timestamped Drive tree. | The review package needed a zoomable diagram set and a concise editable deck without touching excluded Workshop files. | Complete. OfficeCLI validates the local PPTX and all six regenerated BRD/SRS/FRS DOCX files; connector readback confirms the native deck has 24 slides and the Drive tree has its three expected child folders. This is documentation work, not a product defect. | The Drive connector cannot verify public-link inheritance on new child folders (`access_not_verified`); confirm the parent folder's existing public-reader policy in Drive before submitting the review link. |
| CI process issue | PR #145 initially failed the `qa-depth-gate` workflow. | The PR description omitted nine mandatory QA Depth Gate sections; no source, document, or application artifact was invalid. | Fixed by replacing the PR body with all ten required sections and verifying it locally with `node scripts/qa/check-pr-depth.js --stdin` (PASS). This is a process/documentation issue, not a product defect. | Wait for the workflow rerun and read its result before merge; the existing Node deprecation and npm audit warnings are separate non-blocking CI notices. |
| Documentation recovery tooling issue | The first attempt to restore the historical full `AGENTS.md` used a truncated terminal response and restored only 546 lines. | The terminal result was capped before the complete historical file content was available. | Fixed by reading the historical file in sentinel-preserved chunks and restoring all 1,164 historical lines, plus the current rules/OfficeCLI router. This is a tooling/documentation issue, not a product defect. | Verify the restored line count and `qa:agent-rules`; do not add this temporary recovery failure to SAP490 Test and Fix Bug. |

| Lỗi tooling | Sau khi sửa fragment, browser rerun đầu tiên timeout khi chờ `Find Similar Bugs`. | Local UI5 preview service đã start trước khi fragment/bundle đổi nên vẫn serve bundle in-memory cũ. | Đang xử lý. Restart riêng service local của worktree trước khi chạy lại; chưa xem timeout này là product regression khi server mới chưa được kiểm tra. | Verify manifest/fragment đang được serve và chạy lại local browser smoke sau restart. |
| Lỗi tooling | Command restart đầu tiên chưa chạy vì gán vào biến automatic chỉ đọc `$PID` của PowerShell. | Shell dành riêng `$PID` cho process id của chính nó. | Không đổi source hoặc shared-QA state. Retry bằng biến không reserved và inspect port listener thật trước khi dừng. | Restart service local an toàn rồi chạy lại browser smoke. |
| Lỗi UI sản phẩm | Sau concise-label change, browser smoke mới không còn tìm thấy `Find Similar Bugs`. | Fragment custom field dùng `sap.m.Button` trần; pattern custom field hiện có của app cần `SmartAssignmentSection` để kế thừa binding context Fiori. | Đang xử lý. Khôi phục root control có context-aware sẵn có nhưng chỉ giữ button; form vẫn cung cấp label ngắn và không có helper text dài. | Restart local preview rồi chạy lại browser smoke và review screenshot. |
